# Balls to the Wall

Journal: `ep1/quests/minor_quest/mq301_bomb` · `MinorQuest` · hash `3049908432`.

Journal premise: Two BARGHEST pups're in way over their heads and, of course, who else but V to the rescue? I always had a soft spot for tragedies and this one's already got me hooked. You're taking this gig – end of discussion. Think you can pull them outta this shitshow? Got my fingers half-crossed.

**Coverage: journal-linked.** 147 exact journal-linked nodes in 14 resources; 18 resources in the inspected dependency context.

Resource roles in that context: 17 content candidate, 1 repair. Role labels describe path/name evidence, not proof of runtime reachability.

## Required acceptance cases before reuse

Exercise normal completion and each named alternate outcome; interrupt and return at every scene handoff; start with spatial/item/actor predicates already satisfied; save/load across waits and joins; cancel parallel listeners; verify reward delivery once; and verify cleanup without changing the committed outcome. Apply the block-specific cases in the [expanded contracts](../all-quest-blocks.md). No installed playtest is claimed.

1388 repeated typed/socket path signatures in this selected graph context; 255 distinct direct resource identities; 13 embedded graph handoffs. The full occurrence lists, concrete parameters, exact source pointers and external edges are retained in the checked evidence. Shared forward dependencies can serve several quests; these are context counts.

## Scene performance events

Section event data retains actor/animation/audio bindings and authored time fields, with raw pointers. Events in different sections are not treated as one chronological timeline: parent routing, interruption and playback policy still apply. Animation/voice payloads and installed timing are not validated by this extraction.
